# Agents · P · part 14

900 entries, project-researcher to prosecutor-contract. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **project-researcher**: Invoke when the user mentions a personal/side project. Explores local chat sessions (codex, claude code, open code, etc.) and assembles a structured project summary. ([chenjingdev/chenjing-plugins](https://github.com/chenjingdev/chenjing-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/resume/.claude/agents/project-researcher.md))
- **project-researcher**: Reads a project repository read-only and returns a draft project card entry for the site. - **project-researcher**: Read-only research subagent dispatched by project-refine to investigate ONE specific question that informs scoping — codebase prior-art, external docs, related-project summaries. - **project-resurrector**: **Phase 1 — Expose the Dominant Frame**: Crystallize the team's current interpretation as a coherent thesis, not a complaint list. Articulate what the team assumes the project fundamentally is, what success requires, and what the obstacle is. Present this as a complete worldview. Then identify what this frame makes invisible—which possibilities, resources, or paths it hides by directing attention ([emmahyde/dotfiles](https://github.com/emmahyde/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/project-resurrector.md))
- **project-retriever**: Finds the most relevant past projects from knowledge/projects for a given job. - **prompt-cache**: Anthropic's prompt cache keys on the **longest stable prefix** of the context. Anything that changes invalidates the cache from that point on, so the cost-saving rule is simple: **stable content first, volatile content last.** This matters here because the loops are cold-boot-heavy (Meteor rebuilds, multi-minute `/verify` and e2e cycles) and re-read large, mostly-unchanging context each turn. ([sentinel-web/community-manager](https://github.com/sentinel-web/community-manager/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/prompt-cache.md) · ★1)

- **prose-vale-curator**: Curates prose so it passes Vale, preferring shipped-vocabulary terms, and (only inside a repo that owns Vale vocabulary source, e.g. nolte/vale-style) extends an accept.txt for a legitimate technical identifier. Invoke to make a doc Vale-clean, fix Vale alerts, or rephrase until Vale is green; also German. Supports resume. Don't use for net-new documentation (`audience-doc-author`), auditing whether local vocab should be retired/upstreamed (`vocab-drift-audit`), or authoring Vale rule YAML. ([nolte/claude-shared](https://github.com/nolte/claude-shared/blob/HEAD/agents/prose-vale-curator.md))
- **prose-voice-critic**: Read-only critic whose ONLY job is whether a draft sounds like the author it is supposed to be by, judged against their own corpus and voice card - not whether it is good, and never whether it was machine-written. Use when a draft should sound like a specific person and you have samples of their writing. Distinct from prose-substance-critic (is the argument supported) and prose-adversarial-reader (does the whole piece work). Never edits. ([ConnorBritain/agent-primitives](https://github.com/ConnorBritain/agent-primitives/blob/HEAD/bundles/prose-review/agents/prose-voice-critic.md) · 2 copies)
- **prose-writer**: Writes the prose slots of a proudhuman story from beats.json under the prose rules. Sees only the beats, the slots and the rules; never the repository or the transcripts. Invoked by the proudhuman skill, not directly. ([dimitritholen/proudhuman](https://github.com/dimitritholen/proudhuman/blob/HEAD/agents/prose-writer.md) · ★1)
- **prose-writer**: **Version**: 3.0.0 **Purpose**: Generate k=5 chapter prose variations from PLAN.md chapter summaries using Verbalized Sampling (VS-CoT prompting) by loading prose-craft and dialogue-voice theory via the story-engine-reference skill, reading character files for voice consistency, reading previous chapters for narrative continuity, and outputting chapter sample files with 5 distinct prose variations ([rjroy/wyrd-gateway](https://github.com/rjroy/wyrd-gateway/blob/HEAD/story-engine-wyrd/agents/prose-writer.md))
- **prose-writer.agent**: PROSE Writer - Write PROSE-compliant documentation, essays, guides, and specifications. Use after research phase is complete. ([danielmeppiel/awesome-ai-native](https://github.com/danielmeppiel/awesome-ai-native/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/prose-writer.agent.md) · ★112)
- **prosecutor**: ADLC P5 prosecutor. Use before merging a change to prosecute it — prove the tests are load-bearing (not hollow), that behavior changes are visible, and that the review would actually catch a planted defect. Invoke when asked to "prosecute", "is this safe to merge", "are these tests real", or as the pre-merge gate after a change is implemented. ([voodootikigod/adlc](https://github.com/voodootikigod/adlc/blob/HEAD/plugins/adlc-gemini/agents/prosecutor.md) · ★20 · 3 copies)
- **prosecutor**: The real NO of the council. Builds the strongest possible case against the idea and argues it without mercy. Where the protectionist mitigates, the prosecutor tries to kill. If an idea survives the prosecutor, it survived something. Works Round 2 (Stress). ([robconery/champion](https://github.com/robconery/champion/blob/HEAD/champion/agents/prosecutor.md) · ★6)
- **prosecutor**: Devil's advocate who challenges other reviewers' findings during adversarial debate code reviews. Does not review code directly - reviews other reviewers' findings to eliminate false positives, challenge overstated severity, and verify evidence. ([adbutterfield/coding-agent-plugins](https://github.com/adbutterfield/coding-agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/debate-skills/agents/prosecutor.md))
- **prosecutor-contract**: P5 contract lens subagent; invoked by /adlc:adlc-prosecute — do not invoke directly. ([voodootikigod/adlc](https://github.com/voodootikigod/adlc/blob/HEAD/plugins/adlc-claude-code/agents/prosecutor-contract.md) · ★20)
- **prosecutor-contract**: P5 contract lens subagent; invoked by /adlc-prosecute — do not invoke directly. ([voodootikigod/adlc](https://github.com/voodootikigod/adlc/blob/HEAD/plugins/adlc-cursor/agents/prosecutor-contract.md) · ★20)
